  a=softmax(z)

def run_lbfgs_full(z0, tau_rel, maxfun, maxiter):
  a=softmax(z0); m=max(fft_conv(a)); tau=tau_rel*m
  global _FULL_TAU
  _FULL_TAU = max(1e-12, tau)

  z=np.clip(z, -B, B)
  a=softmax(z)
  fa=rfft(a,N)
  c=irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L].real; c=np.maximum(c,0)

def _make_basis(n, kper=8):
  x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/float(n)
  cols=[x-0.5]

  for m in range(1,kper+1): cols.append(np.sin(np.pi*m*x)); cols.append(np.cos(np.pi*m*x))

if s<=0 or not finite: return ones/z.size
return e/s
```

Let's design candidate collection and L-BFGS budget:
```

Candidate full L-BFGS function:
```
def _run_lbfgs_full(z0, tau_rel, maxfun=500, maxiter=200):
  z=np.asarray(z0); z=z-mean; z=clip
  a=softmax(z); c=fft_conv(a); m=max(c); tau=tau_rel*max(m,1e-12)
  global _FULL_TAU,_OBJ_SCALE
  _FULL_TAU=tau; _OBJ_SCALE=float(_OPT_N)
  opts={'maxfun':maxfun,'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-8,'pgtol':1e-8,'disp':False}
  try: return minimize(_full_obj_grad,z,jac=True,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=_FULL_BOUNDS,options=opts)
  except: return None
```
Bounds list created in set_state. For n=21840, list of tuples memory ~? 21840*2*? ~1MB. okay.

def _add_candidate(candidates, z, max_keep=40):
  if z is None: return
  z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float).ravel()
  if z.size!=_OPT_N: return
  if not all finite: nan_to_num
  z=clip(z - mean(z), -BOUND, BOUND)
  a=softmax(z)
  sc=fast_score(a)
  if np.isfinite(sc):
     candidates.append([sc,z.copy()])
     candidates.sort(key=lambda x:x[0]) # lambda prohibited! Use function top-level `_score_key`.
     if len(candidates)>max_keep: del candidates[max_keep:]
```

  if arr.size<2: ...

def _fft_conv(a):
  n=a.size; N=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length(); L=2*n-1
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L].real; np.maximum(c,0,out=c); return c
```
This recomputes N each time; overhead small. But for speed, use globals. In objective, a length n. Use global. For safety, if a.size != _OPT_N, fallback. But start candidates always n. Use global.

Detailed `_full_obj_grad`:
```
def _full_obj_grad(z):
   n=_OPT_N
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if z.size != n:
      return 1e100, np.zeros(max(1,z.size))
   z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=_BOUND, neginf=-_BOUND)
   z=np.clip(z,-_BOUND,_BOUND)
   zc=z - np.mean(z)
   a=_softmax(zc)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,_OPT_NFFT)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,_OPT_NFFT)[:_OPT_L].real
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   m=float(c.max())
   tau=float(_FULL_TAU)
   if tau <=0 or not finite: tau=1e-12
   diff=(c-m)/tau; diff=np.clip(diff,-745,0)
   e=np.exp(diff); se=float(np.sum(e))
   if not finite or se<=0: return 1e100, zeros
   p=e/se
   h=_corr_h(a,p) # it recomputes fa? To avoid recompute, we can pass fa. Let's create `_corr_h_with_fa(a,p,fa)` to avoid. Top-level.
```
Define `_corr_h(a,p,fa=None)`: if fa None compute. In objective pass fa.
```
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   h=np.fft.irfft(fp*np.conjugate(fa),N)[:n].real
   hb=float(np.dot(h,a))
   scale=float(_OBJ_SCALE)
   val = 2.0*n*(m + tau*np.log(se))*scale
   g = 4.0*n*scale*a*(h - hb)
   pen=1e-12*scale
   if pen>0: val += 0.5*pen*float(np.dot(z,z)); g = g + pen*z
   if not finite val: ...
   return float(val), g
```
Check `g` factor: score=2nF; dF/dz=2a(h-hb); d score/dz=4n a(h-hb); scale multiply. Yes.

But `hb` = dot(h,a) is scalar. In softmax derivative we need hbar = dot(h,a), not dot(h*a)? Yes hbar = Σ h_i a_i. `h - hb` means subtract scalar from h vector. Correct.

`_make_basis(n)`:
```
def _make_basis(n):
 x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
 cols=[]
 cols.append(x-0.5)
 for m in range(1,9): cols.append(np.sin(np.pi*m*x)); cols.append(np.cos(np.pi*m*x))
 for m in (1,3,5,7,9): cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x))
 for m in range(1,7): cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x))
 B=np.column_stack(cols).astype(float)
 # center each column (remove mean)
 B = B - B.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
 # Maybe zero out tiny columns
 return B
```
Total K=1+16+5+6=28. Good.

- lin 1
- half m=1..12 (sin,cos) =>24
- periodic sine m=1,3,5,7,9,11 =>6
- periodic cos m=1..5 =>5
Total 36. Dot 20k*36=720k. L-BFGS 36 dims okay. Use `for m in range(1,13)` half.
Columns sin/cos may be linearly dependent? fine.
Centering columns with mean. High frequency mean not exactly zero due linspace endpoint? Sine periodic over 0..1 with N inclusive: mean not exactly? center.

Low-dim starts need basis indices. Since basis order known:

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
  budget=max(60,float(budget_s)); t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget-30
  if budget>=1500: n=21840
  elif budget>=800: n=10920
  else: n=4096
  _set_state(n)
  B=_make_basis(n); _SHAPE_BOUNDS=...
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  candidates=[]
  # height
  try: base=height_sequence_1; add resample; add resample reverse
  except: pass
  # fallback block start: z=0
  add_candidate(candidates, np.zeros(n))
  # low-dim starts list
  K=B.shape[1]
  theta0=np.zeros(K)
  starts=[]
  starts.append(theta0)
  # structured
  for sign in (1,-1): ... set hsin1, hsin3 maybe; linear
  for r in range(4): starts.append(rng.normal(0,0.7,K))
  # low-dim search for some starts
  for si,t0 in enumerate(starts):
    if time>deadline-300: break
    theta=t0.copy()
    tau_list = [0.2,0.08,0.03,0.01] if si==0 else [0.1,0.04,0.015]
    for stage,tau_rel in enumerate(tau_list):
       maxfun=500 if si==0 else 200; if stage==0 maxfun=300; ...
       res=_run_lbfgs_shape(theta,tau_rel,maxfun,200)
       if res is not None: theta=res.x
       z=B.dot(theta)
       add_candidate(candidates,z)
       if time>deadline-120: break
  # add direct structured/random z starts
  add random log Fourier 8 starts
  add single mode z (sin hsin odd, periodic, linear) maybe 10
  # quick full L-BFGS on candidates top maybe 12
  candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
  for idx in range(min(12,len(candidates))):
    if time>deadline-240: break
    z0=candidates[idx][1]
    sc0=candidates[idx][0]
    for tau_rel in (0.08, 0.02): maybe if idx<4 else 0.05
       res=_run_lbfgs_full(z0,tau_rel, maxfun=400, maxiter=150)
       if res: add_candidate(res.x)
  # anneal top 4
  candidates.sort
  for idx in range(min(4,len)):
    z=candidates[idx][1]
    for tau_rel in (0.01,0.003,0.001):
       if time>deadline-120: break
       res=_run_lbfgs_full(z,tau_rel, maxfun=800, maxiter=300)
       add_candidate
  # polish best
  candidates.sort
  if candidates and deadline-time>60:
      pol_deadline=deadline-15
      best_z,pol_score=_polish_exact(candidates[0][1], pol_deadline, seed=seed+1)
      add_candidate(best_z)
      # maybe polish second if time
      if len(candidates)>1 and deadline-time>60:
         ...
  candidates.sort
  best_z = candidates[0][1] if any else zeros
  a=softmax(best_z); normalize; validate
  return [float(x) for x in a]
```

def _polish_exact(z0, deadline, seed=1):
   n=_OPT_N
   b=np.asarray(z0,float).ravel().copy()
   b=np.nan_to_num(b,0,BOUND,-BOUND)
   b=np.clip(b - np.mean(b), -BOUND, BOUND)
   a=_softmax(b)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_b=b.copy()
   best_score=float('inf')
   # initial exact score
   m=max(_fft_conv(a)); best_score=2*n*m
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   norm2=float(n)*float(n)
   mau_list=(0.0005,0.002,0.01,0.04)
   eta_base=(1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,0.1,0.3,1.0)
   no=0; it=0; last_lbfgs=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
      c=_fft_conv(a); m=float(c.max()); score=2*n*m
      if score < best_score - 1e-15:
          best_score=score; best_b=b.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); no=0
      # gradient direction
      tau=mau_list[it % 4] * max(m,1e-15)
      diff=np.clip((c-m)/tau,-745,0); e=np.exp(diff); se=e.sum();
      if invalid: break
      p=e/se
      h=_corr_h(a,p,None) # with fa? Need fa from c. We computed fa? In loop we can compute fa once.
```

If optimal needs values smaller than 1e-13 relative, not important. Could set BOUND=50 to allow smaller, but exp(50) okay? Softmax z max 50, min -50, ratio e^-100 ~3e-44, underflow maybe but okay. Use BOUND=50? In objective, exp of z after subtract max; if z range 100, exp(-100) ~3e-44 not underflow until -745. Bounds 50 safe. Larger bound allows sparser. But L-BFGS may drift; penalty. Use BOUND=50. For low-dim z=Bθ with θ bounds 50 can produce z ±K*50 huge; we clip z to 50. Good. But bounds on θ ±50, high dims. Use BOUND=40 maybe. Let's set 40.

If a_i must be nonnegative, softmax positive. Good.

Now, could there be benefit to non-unique scale due evaluator sum protection if sum<0.01? sum=1 safe.

`_set_state(n)`:
```
def _set_state(n):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_NFFT, _OPT_L, _OBJ_SCALE, _OPT_BOUNDS
    _OPT_N=int(n)
    _OPT_L=2*_OPT_N-1
    _OPT_NFFT=1 << (2*_OPT_N - 1).bit_length()
    _OBJ_SCALE=float(_OPT_N)
    _OPT_BOUNDS=[(-_BOUND, _BOUND)] * _OPT_N
```
If n=0? no.

`_conv_and_fa(a)`:
```
def _conv_and_fa(a):
    n=_OPT_N; N=_OPT_NFFT; L=_OPT_L
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L].real
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c,fa
```
`_fft_conv(a): return _conv_and_fa(a)[0]`

`_fast_score(a)`:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s <=1e-12: return inf
    if a.size != _OPT_N: # rescore general? use local n
       n=a.size; N=1<<(2*n-1).bit_length(); L=2*n-1; fa=rfft; c=...
    else: c,_=_conv_and_fa(a)
    m=float(np.max(c)); return float(2*n*m/(s*s))
```
But for softmax sum=1 and size n. General maybe for resampled height? We can have `_sequence_score` compute with own n. But candidates always softmax size n. Use simpler:
```
def _score_normalized(a): n=a.size; c=...; return 2n max
```
No sum. For arbitrary sequence from height, we normalize first in add? We'll convert raw to z via log, so score from softmax. Use fast.
```
def _score_from_softmax(a):
   c,_=_conv_and_fa(a); return 2.0*_OPT_N*float(c.max())
```
Name `_fast_score`. Ensure a sum 1.

`_add_candidate(candidates, z, max_keep=60)`:
```
def _add_candidate(candidates, z, max_keep=60):
   if z is None: return candidates
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if z.size != _OPT_N: return candidates
   z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=_BOUND, neginf=-_BOUND)
   z=np.clip(z - np.mean(z), -_BOUND, _BOUND)
   a=_softmax(z)
   sc=_fast_score(a)
   if np.isfinite(sc):
      candidates.append((float(sc), z.copy()))
      candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
      if len(candidates)>max_keep:
          del candidates[max_keep:]
   return candidates
```
Use tuple, not list. Sorting with many candidates repeatedly; okay.

`_full_obj_grad`: Use global `_FULL_TAU`, `_OBJ_SCALE`, `_OPT_BOUNDS`? no. Need if z length mismatch. Use `fa` from `_conv_and_fa`? That recomputes fa; we need fa for h. Use manual to avoid double.
```
def _full_obj_grad(z):
  n=_OPT_N
  z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
  if z.size != n: return 1e100, np.zeros(max(1,z.size))
  z=np.nan_to_num(...)
  z=np.clip(z,-_BOUND,_BOUND)
  zc=z-np.mean(z)
  a=_softmax(zc)
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,_OPT_NFFT)
  c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,_OPT_NFFT)[:_OPT_L].real
  c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
  m=float(c.max())
  tau=float(_FULL_TAU)
  if tau<=0 or not finite: tau=1e-12
  diff=(c-m)/tau; diff=np.clip(diff,-745,0)
  e=np.exp(diff); se=float(e.sum())
  if not finite or se<=0: return 1e100, zeros
  p=e/se
  fp=np.fft.rfft(p,_OPT_NFFT)
  h=np.fft.irfft(fp*np.conjugate(fa),_OPT_NFFT)[:n].real
  hb=float(np.dot(h,a))
  scale=float(_OBJ_SCALE)
  val=2.0*n*(m + tau*np.log(se))*scale
  g=4.0*n*scale*a*(h-hb)
  pen=1e-12*scale
  val += 0.5*pen*float(np.dot(z,z))
  g = g + pen*z
  if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e100; g=np.zeros(n)
  return float(val), g
```
Do we need project gradient to zero sum? g sum? a*(h-hb) sum = Σ a h - hb Σ a =0. Penalty adds pen z, sum not zero. Good. But L-BFGS on z with gauge; penalty gradient may have nonzero sum, okay.

`_run_lbfgs_full`:
```
def _run_lbfgs_full(z0, tau_rel, maxfun=400, maxiter=150):
   global _FULL_TAU, _OBJ_SCALE
   z=np.asarray(z0,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if z.size != _OPT_N: return None
   z=np.nan_to_num(...); z=clip(z-mean, -BOUND, BOUND)
   a=_softmax(z); c,_=_conv_and_fa(a); m=float(c.max())
   tau=float(tau_rel)*max(m,1e-15)
   if tau<=0 or not finite: tau=1e-12
   _FULL_TAU=tau
   _OBJ_SCALE=float(_OPT_N)
   opts={'maxfun':int(maxfun),'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-8,'pgtol':1e-8,'disp':False}
   try:
      res=minimize(_full_obj_grad,z,jac=True,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=_OPT_BOUNDS,options=opts)
      return res
   except Exception:
      return None
```
Bounds list length n. `ftol` relative; okay.

`_make_basis(n)` as above. Should use `np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)`; for n large memory. Good.

`_shape_obj_grad(theta)`:
Set globals `_SHAPE_B`, `_SHAPE_TAU`, `_SHAPE_LAM`, `_OBJ_SCALE`.
```
def _shape_obj_grad(theta):
  B=_SHAPE_B
  if B is None: return inf,zeros
  K=B.shape[1]; n=_OPT_N
  theta=np.asarray(theta,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
  if theta.size != K: return ...
  theta=np.nan_to_num(...); theta=clip
  z=np.clip(B.dot(theta), -_BOUND, _BOUND)
  a=_softmax(z)
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,_OPT_NFFT)
  c=... max
  tau=_SHAPE_TAU
  ... p h with fa
  hb=dot(h,a)
  aB=B.T @ a
  haB=B.T @ (h*a)
  scale=float(_OBJ_SCALE)
  lam=float(_SHAPE_LAM)
  val=2*n*(m+tau logse)*scale +0.5*lam*scale*dot(theta,theta)
  g=4*n*scale*(haB - hb*aB) + lam*scale*theta
  return val,g
```
Need factor: `haB` = Σ h_i a_i B_ij. `aB` = Σ a_i B_ij. hb scalar. grad=4n(scale) * (haB - hb*aB). Yes.

`_run_lbfgs_shape`:

def _run_lbfgs_shape(theta0, tau_rel, maxfun=400, maxiter=200):

  B=_SHAPE_B
  if B is None: return None
  K=B.shape[1]
  theta=np.asarray(theta0,...); if size !=K return
  theta=nan_to_num/clip
  z=B.dot(theta); a=softmax; c=...
  m=max; tau=tau_rel*max(m,1e-15)
  _SHAPE_TAU=tau; _SHAPE_LAM=1e-4; _OBJ_SCALE=float(_OPT_N)
  # bounds global _SHAPE_BOUNDS set in propose
  opts...
  res=minimize(_shape_obj_grad, theta, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=_SHAPE_BOUNDS, options=opts)
```

`_polish_exact`: We'll implement as detailed. Need use `_run_lbfgs_full` inside but if n small? okay.

```
def _polish_exact(z0, deadline, seed=1):
   n=_OPT_N
   b=np.asarray(z0,dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
   b=np.nan_to_num(...); b=np.clip(b-mean,...)
   a=_softmax(b)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_b=b.copy()
   c,_=_conv_and_fa(a); best_score=2*n*float(c.max())
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   norm2=float(n)*float(n)
   tau_rels=(0.0005,0.002,0.01,0.04)
   etas=(1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,0.1,0.3,1.0)
   no=0; it=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      c,fa=_conv_and_fa(a)
      m=float(c.max()); score=2n*m
      if score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best_b=b.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); no=0
      # choose p
      if it % 5 == 4:
          Kactive = min(_OPT_L, max(8, int(0.001*_OPT_L))) # 0.1% ~65
          if Kactive >= _OPT_L:
             p=np.ones(_OPT_L)/_OPT_L
          else:
             idx=np.argpartition(c, Kactive-1)[:Kactive]
             p=np.zeros(_OPT_L); p[idx]=1.0/Kactive
      else:
          rel=tau_rels[it % 4]
          tau=rel*max(m,1e-15)
          diff=np.clip((c-m)/tau,-745,0); e=np.exp(diff); se=float(e.sum())
          if se<=0 or not finite: break
          p=e/se
      h=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p,_OPT_NFFT)*np.conjugate(fa),_OPT_NFFT)[:n].real
      hb=float(np.dot(h,a))
      g=norm2*a*(h-hb)
      g=np.clip(g,-50,50)
      accepted=False
      # try negative then positive
      for sgn in (-1.0, 0.0, 1.0):? We don't want 0. Use two:
      for sgn in (-1.0, 1.0):
         if accepted: break
         for eta in etas:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1: break
            bt=np.clip(b + sgn*eta*g, -_BOUND, _BOUND)  # sgn=-1 gives b - eta*g
            at=_softmax(bt)
            ct,_=_conv_and_fa(at)
            mt=float(ct.max()); sc=2n*mt
            if sc < score - 1e-14:
                b=bt; a=at; accepted=True; no=0; break
      if not accepted:
          escaped=False
          for s in (0.02,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0):
             if time > deadline-1: break
             bt=np.clip(b + s*rng.standard_normal(n), -_BOUND, _BOUND)
             at=_softmax(bt); ct,_=_conv_and_fa(at); sc=2n*ct.max()
             if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                b=bt; a=at; escaped=True; no=0; break
          if not escaped:
             no += 1
             if no == 30 or no == 80:
                # run short L-BFGS
                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45:
                   res=_run_lbfgs_full(b, 0.005, 300, 100)
                   if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
                       zres=np.asarray(res.x).ravel()
                       if zres.size==n:
                          zres=np.clip(zres-mean(zres),-BOUND,BOUND)
                          ares=_softmax(zres); scres=2n*max(conv)
                          if scres < score:
                             b=zres; a=ares; no=0
             if no == 120:
                b=best_b + 0.3*rng.standard_normal(n)
                b=np.clip(b-mean(b),-BOUND,BOUND); a=_softmax(b); no=0
             if no >= 200: break
      it += 1
      if it % 100 == 0:
         print(f"polish it={it} cur={score:.9f} best={best_score:.9f} left={max(0,deadline-time.monotonic()):.0f}")
   return best_b, float(best_score)
```
Note `sgn=1.0` tries `b + eta*g` (opposite). But if gradient correct, `b - eta*g` should improve; if not, positive may. The line search uses current `score` not best; positive might improve current even if gradient ascent. Good.

In L-BFGS call inside polish, `_run_lbfgs_full` uses global `_OBJ_SCALE` already float(n), okay. It may modify `_FULL_TAU`. Fine.

Potential issue: `_run_lbfgs_full` inside polish may be called while `score` not exact? It uses smooth objective; returns z maybe not improving exact. We check scres < score (current) or best? Use `< best_score` to be safe? If improves current but not best, maybe okay. Use `< score` to move. Then best updated next loop.

`_polish_exact` uses `best_b` and `best_a` copies; no need best_a. At end return best_b.

Proposal detailed:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    import? already imported.
    budget=max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget-30.0
    if budget >= 1500: n=32768
    elif budget >= 800: n=16384
    else: n=4096
    _set_state(n)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    candidates=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    # base zero
    _add_candidate(candidates, np.zeros(n))
    # height_sequence_1
    try:
       h=height_sequence_1
       h=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if h.size>1:
          hpos=np.maximum(np.nan_to_num(h,0,0,0),1e-300)
          x_old=np.linspace(0,1,hpos.size)
          z=np.interp(x, x_old, np.log(hpos))
          _add_candidate(candidates, z)
          hrev=hpos[::-1]
          zrev=np.interp(x, x_old, np.log(hrev)) # x_old reversed? If hrev length same, x_old same but reversed mapping? For sequence reversed, positions correspond old i -> n-1-i, log h_old[old_n-1-i]. Interpolating hrev over x_old (0..1) is okay.
          _add_candidate(candidates, zrev)
    except Exception:
       pass
    # structured z starts
    for amp in (0.8,1.5):
       for m in (1,3,5,7):
          _add_candidate(candidates, amp*np.sin(np.pi*m*x))
          _add_candidate(candidates, amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x))
       _add_candidate(candidates, amp*(x-0.5))
    # random log fourier starts
    for _ in range(10):
       z=np.zeros(n)
       for m in range(1,15):
          sig=0.8/float(m)
          z += sig*rng.standard_normal()*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x)
          z += 0.7*sig*rng.standard_normal()*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x)
       for m in range(1,8):
          sig=0.6/float(m)
          z += sig*rng.standard_normal()*np.sin(np.pi*m*x)
       _add_candidate(candidates,z)
    # low-dim basis
    B=_make_basis(n)
    global _SHAPE_B, _SHAPE_BOUNDS
    _SHAPE_B=B
    K=B.shape[1]
    _SHAPE_BOUNDS=[(-_BOUND,_BOUND)]*K
    # low-dim theta starts
    low_starts=[]
    low_starts.append(np.zeros(K))
    for sign in (1.0,-1.0):
       t=np.zeros(K)
       # indices hsin m=1,3,5?
       for m in (1,3,5,7):
          idx=1+2*(m-1)
          if idx<K: t[idx]=0.7*sign
       low_starts.append(t)
       t2=np.zeros(K); t2[0]=0.9*sign; low_starts.append(t2)
    for _ in range(5): low_starts.append(rng.normal(0,0.7,K))
    # low-dim search
    for si,t0theta in enumerate(low_starts):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 240: break
       theta=t0theta.copy()
       if si == 0:
          taus=[0.20,0.08,0.03,0.012,0.005]
          maxfuns=[500,700,800,800,1000]
       else:
          taus=[0.12,0.05,0.02]
          maxfuns=[300,400,500]
       for stage,tr in enumerate(taus):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180: break
          mf = maxfuns[stage] if stage < len(maxfuns) else 300
          res=_run_lbfgs_shape(theta,tr,mf,200)
          if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
             theta=np.asarray(res.x).ravel()
          z=B.dot(theta)
          _add_candidate(candidates,z)
          # maybe also add reversed z? Since score invariant under reverse? If z reversed, sequence reversed, same score. Not new. But skip.
    # Now candidates sorted. Run full L-BFGS on top candidates
    candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
    # quick refine top 12
    top_quick = min(12, len(candidates))
    for idx in range(top_quick):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 240: break
       z0=candidates[idx][1]
       # one or two tau stages
       for tau_rel in (0.06, 0.02) if idx<4 else (0.05,):
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180: break
           mf=600 if idx<4 else 350
           res=_run_lbfgs_full(z0,tau_rel,mf,200)
           if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
              _add_candidate(candidates,res.x)
    candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
    # anneal top 4
    for idx in range(min(4,len(candidates))):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
       z=candidates[idx][1]
       for tau_rel in (0.012,0.005,0.002):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90: break
          res=_run_lbfgs_full(z,tau_rel,900,300)
          if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
             _add_candidate(candidates,res.x)
    candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
    # polish top one/two
    if candidates and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
       pol_deadline=deadline-20
       zpol, polsc = _polish_exact(candidates[0][1], pol_deadline, seed=int(seed)+1)
       _add_candidate(candidates, zpol)
       candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
       if len(candidates)>1 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
          zpol2, _ = _polish_exact(candidates[1][1], deadline-15, seed=int(seed)+2)
          _add_candidate(candidates,zpol2)
    candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
    best_z = candidates[0][1] if candidates else np.zeros(n)
    a=_softmax(best_z)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not finite or s<0.01: a=np.ones(n)/n else a/=s
    # optional official eval
    try:
       official=evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
       if np.isfinite(official): print(...)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return [float(v) for v in a.tolist()]
```

Time reservations: In low-dim loop, `deadline-240` ensure enough for full. But if budget smaller, adjustments maybe. For 2400, okay. If budget 300, n=4096, deadlines. Need avoid negative maxfun? If budget small, maybe skip phases. Use checks.

Potential bug: In `_add_candidate`, if `z` has nan? handled. If softmax returns uniform due overflow? okay.

Potential bug: In official eval, if `a` length large evaluate might be slow; but only once. If official returns inf due sum? no. We don't adjust based on official. If official is finite and lower than internal, we don't need to find that sequence? We return same a. If official is poor due FFT? unlikely.

Maybe we should call official on top few and choose best by official? But each call O(n^2); too slow. One call enough. Could call on best and second if time, but not necessary. If official different by ~1e-12, no.

Could using n=32768 cause memory/time in `_polish_exact` line search with many `rng.standard_normal(n)` (32768) okay.

Let's review low-dim gradient again with sigma. Let's test with B constant column (if we had): uniform a, h maybe not constant if tau>0; gradient could move. In a case F constant under θ constant direction, gradient zero. B centered so no constant column, but if θ all zeros, z uniform. Good.

Potential numerical issue: In `_shape_obj_grad`, `B.T @ a` for B 36x32768 and a 32768; BLAS efficient. `B.T @ (h*a)` another dot. Good.

Now, maybe adding regularization λ=1e-4 too high? It penalizes θ magnitude; could prevent high log amplitudes. Since θ amplitude 1 is typical, penalty val 0.5*1e-4*1*32768=1.6 relative to score*32768~49152, tiny. Good. Use 1e-5 maybe even less. Set 1e-4 okay.

Full objective penalty 1e-12*scale: z length 32768, dot maybe 32768*var. If z range 5, dot ~8e5, pen scale 3.27e8? val penalty 0.5*3e-8? Wait pen=1e-12*32768=3.3e-8; dot 8e5 =>0.026, negligible vs val 50000. Good.

```python
"""
Search nonnegative coefficient sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a)^2).

Main ideas:
1. Work with a log-softmax representation a_i = softmax(z_i), so all
   values are nonnegative and the sum is fixed to 1.
2. First optimize a low-dimensional log-Fourier / half-wave shape space
   with L-BFGS-B and annealed smooth-maxification of the convolution peak.
3. Use the best low-dimensional shapes (plus the previous best sequence,
   random low-frequency starts, and stepped profiles) as starts for
   full-vector log-softmax L-BFGS-B runs.
4. Finish with an exact active-subgradient line search polish in log-space,
   including periodic random resets and short L-BFGS-B breaks.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_BOUND = 40.0
_SHAPE_BOUND = 5.0

_OPT_N = 0
_OPT_NFFT = 0
_OPT_L = 0
_OPT_BOUNDS = []
_OBJ_SCALE = 1.0

_FULL_TAU = 1e-6

_SHAPE_B = None
_SHAPE_BOUNDS = []
_SHAPE_TAU = 1e-6
_SHAPE_LAM = 1e-4


def _cand_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _set_state(n):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_NFFT, _OPT_L, _OBJ_SCALE, _OPT_BOUNDS
    _OPT_N = int(n)
    _OPT_L = 2 * _OPT_N - 1
    # N >= 2n-1 is enough for both linear convolution and the needed
    # one-sided derivative cross-correlation.
    _OPT_NFFT = 1 << (2 * _OPT_N - 1).bit_length()
    _OBJ_SCALE = float(_OPT_N)
    _OPT_BOUNDS = [(-_BOUND, _BOUND)] * _OPT_N


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=_BOUND, neginf=-_BOUND)
    z = np.clip(z, -_BOUND, _BOUND)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _conv_and_fa(a):
    n = _OPT_N
    N = _OPT_NFFT
    L = _OPT_L
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, fa


def _fft_conv(a):
    return _conv_and_fa(a)[0]


def _fast_score(a):
    # Assumes a is already normalized and has length _OPT_N.
    c, _ = _conv_and_fa(a)
    return float(2.0 * _OPT_N * np.max(c))


def _add_candidate(candidates, z, max_keep=70):
    if z is None:
        return candidates
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size != _OPT_N:
        return candidates
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=_BOUND, neginf=-_BOUND)
    z = np.clip(z - np.mean(z), -_BOUND, _BOUND)
    a = _softmax(z)
    sc = _fast_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        candidates.append((float(sc), z.copy()))
        candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
        if len(candidates) > max_keep:
            del candidates[max_keep:]
    return candidates


def _full_obj_grad(z):
    n = _OPT_N
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size != n:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(max(1, z.size), dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=_BOUND, neginf=-_BOUND)
    z = np.clip(z, -_BOUND, _BOUND)
    zc = z - np.mean(z)

    a = _softmax(zc)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, _OPT_NFFT)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, _OPT_NFFT)[:_OPT_L].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(c))

    tau = float(_FULL_TAU)
    if tau <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 1e-12

    diff = (c - m) / tau
    diff = np.clip(diff, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(diff)
    se = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    p = e / se
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, _OPT_NFFT)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conjugate(fa), _OPT_NFFT)[:n].real

    hb = float(np.dot(h, a))
    scale = float(_OBJ_SCALE)

    val = 2.0 * n * (m + tau * np.log(se)) * scale

    # d(score)/d(z) for softmax parameterization.
    g = 4.0 * n * scale * a * (h - hb)
    g = g - float(np.mean(g))

    # Tiny ridge to remove the softmax gauge ambiguity.
    pen = 1e-12 * scale
    val += 0.5 * pen * float(np.dot(z, z))
    g = g + pen * z

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1.0e100
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), g


def _run_lbfgs_full(z0, tau_rel, maxfun=500, maxiter=200):
    global _FULL_TAU, _OBJ_SCALE
    n = _OPT_N
    z = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size != n:
        return None
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=_BOUND, neginf=-_BOUND)
    z = np.clip(z - np.mean(z), -_BOUND, _BOUND)

    a = _softmax(z)
    c, _ = _conv_and_fa(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    tau = float(tau_rel) * max(m, 1e-15)
    if tau <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 1e-12

    _FULL_TAU = tau
    _OBJ_SCALE = float(n)

    opts = {
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "ftol": 1e-8,
        "pgtol": 1e-8,
        "disp": False,
    }
    try:
        return minimize(
            _full_obj_grad,
            z,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=_OPT_BOUNDS,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return None


def _make_basis(n):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    cols = []

    # Linear trend.
    cols.append(x - 0.5)

    # Half-wave modes. These can express endpoint and center asymmetry well.
    for m in range(1, 13):
        cols.append(np.sin(np.pi * m * x))
        cols.append(np.cos(np.pi * m * x))

    # Periodic odd sine modes, which reduce central convolution peaks.
    for m in (1, 3, 5, 7, 9, 11):
        cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))

    # A few periodic cosine modes for local shaping.
    for m in range(1, 7):
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))

    B = np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64)
    B = B - B.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return B


def _shape_obj_grad(theta):
    B = _SHAPE_B
    if B is None:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)

    K = B.shape[1]
    n = _OPT_N
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if theta.size != K:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(max(1, theta.size), dtype=np.float64)

    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=_SHAPE_BOUND, neginf=-_SHAPE_BOUND)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -_SHAPE_BOUND, _SHAPE_BOUND)

    z = B.dot(theta)
    a = _softmax(z)

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, _OPT_NFFT)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, _OPT_NFFT)[:_OPT_L].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(c))

    tau = float(_SHAPE_TAU)
    if tau <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 1e-12

    diff = (c - m) / tau
    diff = np.clip(diff, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(diff)
    se = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)

    p = e / se
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, _OPT_NFFT)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conjugate(fa), _OPT_NFFT)[:n].real

    hb = float(np.dot(h, a))
    aB = B.T @ a
    haB = B.T @ (h * a)

    scale = float(_OBJ_SCALE)
    lam = float(_SHAPE_LAM)

    val = 2.0 * n * (m + tau * np.log(se)) * scale
    g = 4.0 * n * scale * (haB - hb * aB)

    val += 0.5 * lam * scale * float(np.dot(theta, theta))
    g = g + lam * scale * theta

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1.0e100
        g = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), g


def _run_lbfgs_shape(theta0, tau_rel, maxfun=400, maxiter=200):
    global _SHAPE_TAU, _SHAPE_LAM, _OBJ_SCALE
    B = _SHAPE_B
    if B is None:
        return None

    K = B.shape[1]
    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if theta.size != K:
        return None
    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=_SHAPE_BOUND, neginf=-_SHAPE_BOUND)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -_SHAPE_BOUND, _SHAPE_BOUND)

    z = B.dot(theta)
    a = _softmax(z)
    c, _ = _conv_and_fa(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    tau = float(tau_rel) * max(m, 1e-15)
    if tau <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 1e-12

    _SHAPE_TAU = tau
    _SHAPE_LAM = 1e-4
    _OBJ_SCALE = float(_OPT_N)

    opts = {
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "ftol": 1e-8,
        "pgtol": 1e-8,
        "disp": False,
    }
    try:
        return minimize(
            _shape_obj_grad,
            theta,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=_SHAPE_BOUNDS,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return None


def _polish_exact(z0, deadline, seed=1):
    n = _OPT_N
    b = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=_BOUND, neginf=-_BOUND)
    b = np.clip(b - np.mean(b), -_BOUND, _BOUND)

    a = _softmax(b)
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score(a)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    norm2 = float(n) * float(n)

    tau_rels = (0.0005, 0.002, 0.01, 0.04)
    base_deltas = (1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0)

    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c, fa = _conv_and_fa(a)
        m = float(np.max(c))
        score = 2.0 * n * m

        if score < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_b = b.copy()
            no_improve = 0

        # Active-set or smooth weights.
        if it % 5 == 4:
            Kactive = min(_OPT_L, max(8, int(0.001 * _OPT_L)))
            if Kactive >= _OPT_L:
                p = np.ones(_OPT_L, dtype=np.float64) / float(_OPT_L)
            else:
                idx = np.argpartition(c, Kactive - 1)[:Kactive]
                p = np.zeros(_OPT_L, dtype=np.float64)
                p[idx] = 1.0 / float(Kactive)
        else:
            rel = tau_rels[it % 4]
            tau = rel * max(m, 1e-15)
            diff = (c - m) / tau
            diff = np.clip(diff, -745.0, 0.0)
            e = np.exp(diff)
            se = float(np.sum(e))
            if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
                break
            p = e / se

        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, _OPT_NFFT)
        h = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conjugate(fa), _OPT_NFFT)[:n].real
        hb = float(np.dot(h, a))

        # Scaled log-space gradient direction.
        g = norm2 * a * (h - hb)
        g = np.clip(g, -50.0, 50.0)
        gmax = max(1e-8, float(np.max(np.abs(g))))

        accepted = False
        for sgn in (-1.0, 1.0):
            if accepted:
                break
            for delta in base_deltas:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                    break
                eta = delta / gmax
                if eta > 1.0e4:
                    eta = 1.0e4
                if eta < 1.0e-7:
                    eta = 1.0e-7

                bt = np.clip(b + sgn * eta * g, -_BOUND, _BOUND)
                at = _softmax(bt)
                ct, _ = _conv_and_fa(at)
                sc = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(ct))

                if sc < score - 1e-14:
                    b = bt
                    a = at
                    accepted = True
                    no_improve = 0
                    break

        if not accepted:
            escaped = False
            for s in (0.02, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                    break
                bt = np.clip(b + s * rng.standard_normal(n), -_BOUND, _BOUND)
                at = _softmax(bt)
                ct, _ = _conv_and_fa(at)
                sc = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(ct))
                if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                    b = bt
                    a = at
                    escaped = True
                    no_improve = 0
                    break

            if not escaped:
                no_improve += 1

                if no_improve == 30 or no_improve == 80:
                    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
                        res = _run_lbfgs_full(b, 0.005, 300, 100)
                        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
                            zres = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                            if zres.size == n:
                                zres = np.nan_to_num(zres, nan=0.0, posinf=_BOUND, neginf=-_BOUND)
                                zres = np.clip(zres - np.mean(zres), -_BOUND, _BOUND)
                                ares = _softmax(zres)
                                scres = _fast_score(ares)
                                if scres < score:
                                    b = zres
                                    a = ares
                                    no_improve = 0

                if no_improve == 120:
                    b = best_b + 0.3 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=_BOUND, neginf=-_BOUND)
                    b = np.clip(b - np.mean(b), -_BOUND, _BOUND)
                    a = _softmax(b)
                    no_improve = 0

                if no_improve >= 200:
                    break

        it += 1
        if it % 100 == 0:
            left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
            print(
                f"polish it={it} current={score:.9f} "
                f"best={best_score:.9f} left={left:.0f}s"
            )

    return best_b, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Return the best nonnegative coefficient list found under the time budget.
    """
    budget_s = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if budget_s > 600.0:
        reserve = 120.0
    else:
        reserve = 30.0
    deadline = t0 + max(30.0, budget_s - reserve)

    if budget_s >= 1800.0:
        n = 32768
    elif budget_s >= 900.0:
        n = 16384
    else:
        n = 4096

    _set_state(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    candidates = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    # Trivial fallback.
    _add_candidate(candidates, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))

    # Start from the previously known best construction, if available.
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if h.size > 1:
                h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                h = np.maximum(h, 1e-300)
                x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h.size)
                old_log = np.log(h)

                z = np.interp(x, x_old, old_log)
                _add_candidate(candidates, z)

                hrev = h[::-1]
                zrev = np.interp(x, x_old, np.log(hrev))
                _add_candidate(candidates, zrev)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Structured full-vector log-space starts.
    for amp in (0.8, 1.5, 2.2):
        for m in (1, 3, 5, 7):
            _add_candidate(candidates, amp * np.sin(np.pi * m * x))
            _add_candidate(candidates, amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))
        _add_candidate(candidates, amp * (x - 0.5))

    for shift in (0.35, 0.5, 0.65):
        zstep = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        zstep[:, None]  # no-op to keep numpy happy? This line unnecessary but harmless.
        zstep = np.where(x < shift, 1.3, -1.3).astype(np.float64)
        _add_candidate(candidates, zstep)

    # Random smooth Fourier perturbations.
    for _ in range(10):
        zrand = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 15):
            sig = 0.85 / float(m)
            zrand += sig * rng.standard_normal() * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x)
            zrand += 0.72 * sig * rng.standard_normal() * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x)
        for m in range(1, 8):
            sig = 0.65 / float(m)
            zrand += sig * rng.standard_normal() * np.sin(np.pi * m * x)
        _add_candidate(candidates, zrand)

    # Low-dimensional shape space.
    global _SHAPE_B, _SHAPE_BOUNDS
    _SHAPE_B = _make_basis(n)
    K = _SHAPE_B.shape[1]
    _SHAPE_BOUNDS = [(-_SHAPE_BOUND, _SHAPE_BOUND)] * K

    low_starts = []
    low_starts.append(np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))

    for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
        # Antisymmetric half-wave sine modes.
        t = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in (1, 3, 5, 7):
            idx = 1 + 2 * (m - 1)
            if idx < K:
                t[idx] = 0.75 * sign
        low_starts.append(t)

        # Linear antisymmetric-ish trend.
        t2 = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        t2[0] = 0.95 * sign
        low_starts.append(t2)

        # Periodic odd sine modes.
        t3 = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        base_psin = 1 + 24
        if base_psin < K:
            for j, m in enumerate((1, 3, 5, 7)):
                if base_psin + j < K:
                    t3[base_psin + j] = 0.65 * sign
        low_starts.append(t3)

    for _ in range(5):
        low_starts.append(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, size=K).astype(np.float64))

    # Low-dimensional optimization with tau continuation.
    for si, theta0 in enumerate(low_starts):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 240.0:
            break

        theta = theta0.copy()
        if si == 0:
            taus = (0.20, 0.08, 0.035, 0.015, 0.007)
            maxfuns = (500, 700, 800, 900, 1000)
        else:
            taus = (0.12, 0.055, 0.022)
            maxfuns = (300, 450, 550)

        for stage, tr in enumerate(taus):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180.0:
                break
            mf = maxfuns[stage] if stage < len(maxfuns) else 300
            res = _run_lbfgs_shape(theta, tr, maxfun=mf, maxiter=220)
            if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
                theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            z = _SHAPE_B.dot(theta)
            _add_candidate(candidates, z)

    if candidates:
        candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
        print(
            f"after lowdim/n={_OPT_N} best_internal={candidates[0][0]:.9f} "
            f"elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s"
        )

    # Quick full-vector refinement of the strongest smooth shapes.
    quick_pool = []
    for item in candidates[:12]:
        quick_pool.append(item)

    for idx, (sc0, z0) in enumerate(quick_pool):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 240.0:
            break
        if idx < 4:
            taus = (0.06, 0.022)
            mf = 700
        else:
            taus = (0.05,)
            mf = 400
        for tr in taus:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180.0:
                break
            res = _run_lbfgs_full(z0, tr, maxfun=mf, maxiter=260)
            if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
                _add_candidate(candidates, res.x)

    candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
    if candidates:
        print(
            f"after quick full best_internal={candidates[0][0]:.9f} "
            f"elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s"
        )

    # Anneal the very best few candidates to exact quasi-convergence.
    anneal_pool = []
    for item in candidates[:4]:
        anneal_pool.append(item)

    for idx, (sc0, z0) in enumerate(anneal_pool):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
            break
        for tr in (0.014, 0.006, 0.0025):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
                break
            res = _run_lbfgs_full(z0, tr, maxfun=900, maxiter=350)
            if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
                _add_candidate(candidates, res.x)

    candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
    if candidates:
        print(
            f"after anneal best_internal={candidates[0][0]:.9f} "
            f"elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s"
        )

    # Exact active-subgradient polish.
    if candidates and time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
        pol_deadline = deadline - 20.0
        zpol, scpol = _polish_exact(candidates[0][1], pol_deadline, seed=int(seed) + 1)
        _add_candidate(candidates, zpol)
        candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
        if candidates:
            print(
                f"after polish1 best_internal={candidates[0][0]:.9f} "
                f"elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s"
            )

        if len(candidates) > 1 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
            zpol2, _ = _polish_exact(candidates[1][1], deadline - 10.0, seed=int(seed) + 2)
            _add_candidate(candidates, zpol2)
            candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)

    if candidates:
        best_z = candidates[0][1]
        best_internal = candidates[0][0]
    else:
        best_z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_internal = 2.0

    a = _softmax(best_z)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = a / s

    # One official sanity check, if there is clearly time left.  The returned
    # sequence is based on the internal exact score; the official call is only
    # for logging.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline + 30.0:
        try:
            official = evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
            if np.isfinite(official):
                print(f"official evaluator score={float(official):.9f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"final returned internal score={float(best_internal):.9f}, length={n}")
    return [float(v) for v in a.tolist()]
```
